Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The metals variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 929 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 796 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_929(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 929. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^796(S, F) is trivial. QED.
